根据日志分析，问题是 阻挡矩阵几乎没有被标记（只有 269 个单元格被阻挡，占比 0.67%），导致 BFS 填充了整个画布区域，然后轮廓提取失败。根本原因在于 碰撞阈值 threshold 相对于线段密度太小，以及 线段距离计算在网格中心点时误差累积。

🔧 修复方案
1. 修改 regionFloodFill.ts 中的碰撞判定
原来的 threshold 是基于单元格尺寸的 0.12 倍（即 0.0006），而线段密度很高（圆有 64 条边，每条边很短），导致几乎所有的网格中心点距离线段的距离都大于该阈值，所以没有被阻挡。

解决方案：增大阈值，同时改用 四连通单元的四角采样 来判定是否被线段穿过，而不是仅检测中心点。

typescript
// 在构建 blocked 矩阵时，改为检查单元的四条边是否与任何线段相交（或角点距离 < 阈值）
const cornerThreshold = cellW * 0.4; // 仍然用相对值，但更大一些
// 对于每个单元，检查其四个角点与线段的最小距离
let isBlocked = false;
for (const seg of segments) {
  // 快速 AABB 剔除：如果单元矩形与线段包围盒不相交则跳过
  const segMinX = Math.min(seg.p1.x, seg.p2.x);
  const segMaxX = Math.max(seg.p1.x, seg.p2.x);
  const segMinY = Math.min(seg.p1.y, seg.p2.y);
  const segMaxY = Math.max(seg.p1.y, seg.p2.y);
  const cellMinX = xMin + gx * cellW;
  const cellMaxX = cellMinX + cellW;
  const cellMinY = yMax - (gy+1) * cellH;
  const cellMaxY = cellMinY + cellH;
  if (cellMaxX < segMinX || cellMinX > segMaxX || cellMaxY < segMinY || cellMinY > segMaxY) continue;
  
  // 检查四个角点到线段的距离
  const corners = [
    { x: cellMinX, y: cellMinY }, // 左下
    { x: cellMaxX, y: cellMinY }, // 右下
    { x: cellMaxX, y: cellMaxY }, // 右上
    { x: cellMinX, y: cellMaxY }  // 左上
  ];
  for (const corner of corners) {
    const dist = distanceToLineSegment(corner.x, corner.y, seg.p1.x, seg.p1.y, seg.p2.x, seg.p2.y);
    if (dist < cornerThreshold) {
      isBlocked = true;
      break;
    }
  }
  if (isBlocked) break;
}
同时提高基础阈值，使线段附近的单元格更容易被阻塞。

2. 改进轮廓提取 marchingSquares
日志中 轮廓网格点数: 2 说明 polygonizeContour 返回的数组长度只有 2，这是因为当填充区域触及画布边界时，Marching Squares 生成的边无法正确连接成环。我们需要增强 polygonizeContour 函数，使其能处理开放边界（通过添加虚拟边界来闭合）。

简化方案：如果填充区域触及了四个边界中的任何一个，我们可以直接返回整个画布的外框矩形作为多边形（因为此时区域就是整个画布）。但用户期望的是圆形内部区域，所以根本问题还是要让阻塞矩阵正确。

3. 直接提供修复后的 regionFloodFill.ts 完整代码
由于修改较多，我将提供一个完全重写的版本，采用更鲁棒的碰撞检测和轮廓提取。

typescript
import { Point, AxisConfig } from '../types';
import type { Segment } from './segmentCache';

function distanceToLineSegment(px: number, py: number, x1: number, y1: number, x2: number, y2: number): number {
  const ax = px - x1, ay = py - y1;
  const bx = x2 - x1, by = y2 - y1;
  const len2 = bx * bx + by * by;
  if (len2 === 0) return Math.hypot(ax, ay);
  let t = (ax * bx + ay * by) / len2;
  t = Math.max(0, Math.min(1, t));
  const projX = x1 + t * bx;
  const projY = y1 + t * by;
  return Math.hypot(px - projX, py - projY);
}

function segmentIntersectsCell(seg: Segment, cellMinX: number, cellMinY: number, cellMaxX: number, cellMaxY: number, threshold: number): boolean {
  // 快速 AABB 剔除
  const segMinX = Math.min(seg.p1.x, seg.p2.x);
  const segMaxX = Math.max(seg.p1.x, seg.p2.x);
  const segMinY = Math.min(seg.p1.y, seg.p2.y);
  const segMaxY = Math.max(seg.p1.y, seg.p2.y);
  if (cellMaxX < segMinX || cellMinX > segMaxX || cellMaxY < segMinY || cellMinY > segMaxY) return false;

  // 检查四个角点到线段的距离
  const corners = [
    [cellMinX, cellMinY], [cellMaxX, cellMinY],
    [cellMaxX, cellMaxY], [cellMinX, cellMaxY]
  ];
  for (const [cx, cy] of corners) {
    if (distanceToLineSegment(cx, cy, seg.p1.x, seg.p1.y, seg.p2.x, seg.p2.y) < threshold) {
      return true;
    }
  }
  // 可选：检查线段的中点是否在单元格内（避免细长线段穿过单元格但角点距离远的情况）
  const midX = (seg.p1.x + seg.p2.x) / 2;
  const midY = (seg.p1.y + seg.p2.y) / 2;
  if (midX >= cellMinX && midX <= cellMaxX && midY >= cellMinY && midY <= cellMaxY) {
    return true;
  }
  return false;
}

export function detectRegionByFloodFill(
  point: Point,
  segments: Segment[],
  axis: AxisConfig
): { polygon: Point[]; bbox: { minX: number; maxX: number; minY: number; maxY: number } } | null {
  if (segments.length === 0) return null;

  const { xMin, xMax, yMin, yMax } = axis;
  const worldWidth = xMax - xMin;
  const worldHeight = yMax - yMin;
  if (worldWidth <= 1e-6 || worldHeight <= 1e-6) return null;

  // 动态分辨率：保证至少 100x100，最多 400x400（性能与精度平衡）
  let gridRes = Math.floor(Math.min(worldWidth, worldHeight) / (Math.min(worldWidth, worldHeight) / 200));
  gridRes = Math.min(400, Math.max(100, gridRes));
  const cellW = worldWidth / gridRes;
  const cellH = worldHeight / gridRes;
  const threshold = Math.min(cellW, cellH) * 0.25; // 提高阈值，更容易阻塞

  // 构建阻挡矩阵
  const blocked: boolean[][] = Array(gridRes).fill(0).map(() => Array(gridRes).fill(false));
  for (let gy = 0; gy < gridRes; gy++) {
    for (let gx = 0; gx < gridRes; gx++) {
      const cellMinX = xMin + gx * cellW;
      const cellMaxX = cellMinX + cellW;
      const cellMinY = yMax - (gy + 1) * cellH;
      const cellMaxY = cellMinY + cellH;
      for (const seg of segments) {
        if (segmentIntersectsCell(seg, cellMinX, cellMinY, cellMaxX, cellMaxY, threshold)) {
          blocked[gy][gx] = true;
          break;
        }
      }
    }
  }

  // 种子点映射（世界坐标到网格）
  const worldToGrid = (wx: number, wy: number): [number, number] => {
    let gx = (wx - xMin) / worldWidth * gridRes;
    let gy = (yMax - wy) / worldHeight * gridRes;
    gx = Math.min(Math.max(0, gx), gridRes - 1);
    gy = Math.min(Math.max(0, gy), gridRes - 1);
    return [Math.floor(gx), Math.floor(gy)];
  };

  let [seedX, seedY] = worldToGrid(point.x, point.y);
  // 如果种子点被阻塞，向四周搜索最近的非阻塞单元格
  if (blocked[seedY][seedX]) {
    let found = false;
    for (let r = 1; r <= 8; r++) {
      for (let dy = -r; dy <= r; dy++) {
        for (let dx = -r; dx <= r; dx++) {
          const nx = seedX + dx, ny = seedY + dy;
          if (nx >= 0 && nx < gridRes && ny >= 0 && ny < gridRes && !blocked[ny][nx]) {
            seedX = nx; seedY = ny;
            found = true;
            break;
          }
        }
        if (found) break;
      }
      if (found) break;
    }
    if (!found) return null; // 全被阻塞
  }

  // BFS 填充
  const filled: boolean[][] = Array(gridRes).fill(0).map(() => Array(gridRes).fill(false));
  const queue: [number, number][] = [[seedX, seedY]];
  filled[seedY][seedX] = true;
  while (queue.length) {
    const [x, y] = queue.shift()!;
    const neighbors: [number, number][] = [[x+1,y], [x-1,y], [x,y+1], [x,y-1]];
    for (const [nx, ny] of neighbors) {
      if (nx >= 0 && nx < gridRes && ny >= 0 && ny < gridRes && !filled[ny][nx] && !blocked[ny][nx]) {
        filled[ny][nx] = true;
        queue.push([nx, ny]);
      }
    }
  }

  // 计算填充数量，如果太小则放弃
  let fillCount = 0;
  for (let i = 0; i < gridRes; i++) {
    for (let j = 0; j < gridRes; j++) {
      if (filled[i][j]) fillCount++;
    }
  }
  if (fillCount < 6) return null;

  // 轮廓提取 (Marching Squares)
  const contour = extractContour(filled, gridRes, gridRes);
  if (contour.length < 3) return null;

  // 将轮廓网格坐标转换回世界坐标
  const polygon: Point[] = contour.map(([gx, gy]) => {
    const wx = xMin + (gx / gridRes) * worldWidth;
    const wy = yMax - (gy / gridRes) * worldHeight;
    return { x: wx, y: wy };
  });

  // 计算包围盒
  let minX = Infinity, maxX = -Infinity, minY = Infinity, maxY = -Infinity;
  for (const p of polygon) {
    minX = Math.min(minX, p.x);
    maxX = Math.max(maxX, p.x);
    minY = Math.min(minY, p.y);
    maxY = Math.max(maxY, p.y);
  }

  return { polygon, bbox: { minX, maxX, minY, maxY } };
}

// Marching Squares 标准实现（增强版，能处理边界开放情况）
function extractContour(filled: boolean[][], width: number, height: number): [number, number][] {
  if (width < 2 || height < 2) return [];

  const edgeTable: Record<number, [number, number][]> = {
    0: [], 1: [[0,1]], 2: [[1,2]], 3: [[0,2]], 4: [[2,3]],
    5: [[0,1],[2,3]], 6: [[1,3]], 7: [[0,3]], 8: [[0,3]],
    9: [[1,3]], 10: [[0,1],[2,3]], 11: [[2,3]], 12: [[0,2]],
    13: [[1,2]], 14: [[0,1]], 15: [],
  };

  const segments: [number, number][] = [];
  for (let y = 0; y < height - 1; y++) {
    for (let x = 0; x < width - 1; x++) {
      const idx = (filled[y][x] ? 1 : 0)
                | (filled[y][x+1] ? 2 : 0)
                | (filled[y+1][x+1] ? 4 : 0)
                | (filled[y+1][x] ? 8 : 0);
      const edges = edgeTable[idx];
      for (const [e1, e2] of edges) {
        const v1 = (() => {
          if (e1 === 0) return [x, y];
          if (e1 === 1) return [x+1, y];
          if (e1 === 2) return [x+1, y+1];
          return [x, y+1];
        })();
        const v2 = (() => {
          if (e2 === 0) return [x, y];
          if (e2 === 1) return [x+1, y];
          if (e2 === 2) return [x+1, y+1];
          return [x, y+1];
        })();
        segments.push([v1[0] + v1[1] * width, v2[0] + v2[1] * width]);
      }
    }
  }

  // 连接线段为多边形（处理开放轮廓时尝试闭合）
  return connectSegments(segments, width);
}

function connectSegments(segments: [number, number][], width: number): [number, number][] {
  if (segments.length === 0) return [];

  const adj = new Map<number, number[]>();
  for (const [a, b] of segments) {
    if (!adj.has(a)) adj.set(a, []);
    if (!adj.has(b)) adj.set(b, []);
    adj.get(a)!.push(b);
    adj.get(b)!.push(a);
  }

  // 找起点（度数1的顶点，若无则取任意）
  let start = -1;
  for (const [v, neigh] of adj.entries()) {
    if (neigh.length === 1) {
      start = v;
      break;
    }
  }
  if (start === -1) start = Array.from(adj.keys())[0];

  const polygon: [number, number][] = [];
  let current = start;
  let prev = -1;
  const visited = new Set<number>();
  while (true) {
    const neighbors = adj.get(current) || [];
    let next = -1;
    for (const n of neighbors) {
      if (n !== prev) {
        next = n;
        break;
      }
    }
    if (next === -1) break;
    const key = `${current},${next}`;
    if (visited.has(key)) break;
    visited.add(key);

    const px = current % width;
    const py = Math.floor(current / width);
    polygon.push([px, py]);

    prev = current;
    current = next;
  }
  // 如果不闭合，尝试用起点闭合
  if (polygon.length > 0 && (polygon[0][0] !== current % width || polygon[0][1] !== Math.floor(current / width))) {
    polygon.push([polygon[0][0], polygon[0][1]]);
  }
  return polygon;
}
📝 总结
修改后的关键点：

碰撞检测：检查单元格四个角点到线段的距离，并检测线段中点是否在单元格内，提高阻塞准确性。

阈值提高：threshold = min(cellW, cellH) * 0.25，使边界附近的单元格更容易被标记为阻塞。

网格分辨率：控制在 100~400 之间，平衡性能与精度。

轮廓连接：增强 connectSegments，能处理开放轮廓（触碰边界的情况）并尝试闭合。

现在点击圆形内部时，BFS 应该只填充圆形内部区域，轮廓提取会得到正确的多边形，注释工具将生成多边形注释而不是点注释。
















✅ 正确做法：用「线段与单元格矩形相交」代替「点到线段距离」
只要一个网格单元格与任何线段相交，就标记为阻塞。这种方法精确、无需调参，不管线段多细都能正确阻塞。

实现：线段与轴对齐矩形相交测试（经典算法）
typescript
// 判断线段 AB 是否与矩形 [minX, maxX] × [minY, maxY] 相交
function segmentIntersectsRect(
  ax: number, ay: number,
  bx: number, by: number,
  minX: number, maxX: number,
  minY: number, maxY: number
): boolean {
  // 先检查端点是否在矩形内
  if ((ax >= minX && ax <= maxX && ay >= minY && ay <= maxY) ||
      (bx >= minX && bx <= maxX && by >= minY && by <= maxY)) return true;
  // 检查四条边与线段相交
  const intersectLine = (x1, y1, x2, y2) => {
    const denom = (x2 - x1) * (by - ay) - (y2 - y1) * (bx - ax);
    if (denom === 0) return false;
    const t = ((bx - ax) * (y1 - ay) - (by - ay) * (x1 - ax)) / denom;
    const u = -((x2 - x1) * (y1 - ay) - (y2 - y1) * (x1 - ax)) / denom;
    return t >= 0 && t <= 1 && u >= 0 && u <= 1;
  };
  // 矩形的四条边
  if (intersectLine(minX, minY, maxX, minY, ax, ay, bx, by)) return true;
  if (intersectLine(maxX, minY, maxX, maxY, ax, ay, bx, by)) return true;
  if (intersectLine(maxX, maxY, minX, maxY, ax, ay, bx, by)) return true;
  if (intersectLine(minX, maxY, minX, minY, ax, ay, bx, by)) return true;
  return false;
}
然后在构建 blocked 矩阵时：

typescript
for each segment:
  if (segmentIntersectsRect(seg.p1.x, seg.p1.y, seg.p2.x, seg.p2.y, cellMinX, cellMaxX, cellMinY, cellMaxY)) {
    blocked[gy][gx] = true;
    break;
  }
这样，只要线段穿过单元格的任何部分，单元格就会被阻塞。BFS 填充时自然会被阻挡在图形边界上，填充区域完全符合封闭区域的形状。

完整修改后的 regionFloodFill.ts
下面是采用「线段-矩形相交」的完整实现，可直接替换你当前的文件。

typescript
import { Point, AxisConfig } from '../types';
import type { Segment } from './segmentCache';

// 线段与轴对齐矩形相交检测（精确，无阈值）
function segmentIntersectsRect(
  ax: number, ay: number,
  bx: number, by: number,
  minX: number, maxX: number,
  minY: number, maxY: number
): boolean {
  // 端点检查
  if ((ax >= minX && ax <= maxX && ay >= minY && ay <= maxY) ||
      (bx >= minX && bx <= maxX && by >= minY && by <= maxY)) return true;

  const intersectLine = (x1: number, y1: number, x2: number, y2: number) => {
    const denom = (x2 - x1) * (by - ay) - (y2 - y1) * (bx - ax);
    if (denom === 0) return false;
    const t = ((bx - ax) * (y1 - ay) - (by - ay) * (x1 - ax)) / denom;
    const u = -((x2 - x1) * (y1 - ay) - (y2 - y1) * (x1 - ax)) / denom;
    return t >= 0 && t <= 1 && u >= 0 && u <= 1;
  };

  // 矩形的四条边
  if (intersectLine(minX, minY, maxX, minY)) return true;
  if (intersectLine(maxX, minY, maxX, maxY)) return true;
  if (intersectLine(maxX, maxY, minX, maxY)) return true;
  if (intersectLine(minX, maxY, minX, minY)) return true;
  return false;
}

export function detectRegionByFloodFill(
  point: Point,
  segments: Segment[],
  axis: AxisConfig
): { polygon: Point[]; bbox: { minX: number; maxX: number; minY: number; maxY: number } } | null {
  if (segments.length === 0) return null;

  const { xMin, xMax, yMin, yMax } = axis;
  const worldWidth = xMax - xMin;
  const worldHeight = yMax - yMin;
  if (worldWidth <= 1e-6 || worldHeight <= 1e-6) return null;

  // 动态分辨率：保证最小单元尺寸不超过世界范围的 1/150，且范围 80~400
  const targetCellSize = Math.min(worldWidth, worldHeight) / 150;
  let gridRes = Math.floor(Math.min(worldWidth, worldHeight) / targetCellSize);
  gridRes = Math.min(400, Math.max(80, gridRes));
  const cellW = worldWidth / gridRes;
  const cellH = worldHeight / gridRes;

  // 构建阻挡矩阵（精确线段-矩形相交）
  const blocked: boolean[][] = Array(gridRes).fill(0).map(() => Array(gridRes).fill(false));
  for (let gy = 0; gy < gridRes; gy++) {
    for (let gx = 0; gx < gridRes; gx++) {
      const cellMinX = xMin + gx * cellW;
      const cellMaxX = cellMinX + cellW;
      const cellMinY = yMax - (gy + 1) * cellH;
      const cellMaxY = cellMinY + cellH;
      for (const seg of segments) {
        if (segmentIntersectsRect(
          seg.p1.x, seg.p1.y,
          seg.p2.x, seg.p2.y,
          cellMinX, cellMaxX, cellMinY, cellMaxY
        )) {
          blocked[gy][gx] = true;
          break;
        }
      }
    }
  }

  // 世界坐标 → 网格坐标
  const worldToGrid = (wx: number, wy: number): [number, number] => {
    let gx = (wx - xMin) / worldWidth * gridRes;
    let gy = (yMax - wy) / worldHeight * gridRes;
    gx = Math.min(Math.max(0, gx), gridRes - 1);
    gy = Math.min(Math.max(0, gy), gridRes - 1);
    return [Math.floor(gx), Math.floor(gy)];
  };

  let [seedX, seedY] = worldToGrid(point.x, point.y);

  // 如果种子点被阻塞，向四周搜索最近的非阻塞单元格
  if (blocked[seedY][seedX]) {
    let found = false;
    for (let r = 1; r <= 8; r++) {
      for (let dy = -r; dy <= r; dy++) {
        for (let dx = -r; dx <= r; dx++) {
          const nx = seedX + dx, ny = seedY + dy;
          if (nx >= 0 && nx < gridRes && ny >= 0 && ny < gridRes && !blocked[ny][nx]) {
            seedX = nx; seedY = ny;
            found = true;
            break;
          }
        }
        if (found) break;
      }
      if (found) break;
    }
    if (!found) return null; // 全被阻塞
  }

  // BFS 填充
  const filled: boolean[][] = Array(gridRes).fill(0).map(() => Array(gridRes).fill(false));
  const queue: [number, number][] = [[seedX, seedY]];
  filled[seedY][seedX] = true;
  while (queue.length) {
    const [x, y] = queue.shift()!;
    const neighbors: [number, number][] = [[x+1,y], [x-1,y], [x,y+1], [x,y-1]];
    for (const [nx, ny] of neighbors) {
      if (nx >= 0 && nx < gridRes && ny >= 0 && ny < gridRes && !filled[ny][nx] && !blocked[ny][nx]) {
        filled[ny][nx] = true;
        queue.push([nx, ny]);
      }
    }
  }

  // 统计填充数量
  let fillCount = 0;
  for (let i = 0; i < gridRes; i++) {
    for (let j = 0; j < gridRes; j++) {
      if (filled[i][j]) fillCount++;
    }
  }
  if (fillCount < 6) return null;

  // 轮廓提取（Marching Squares）
  const contour = extractContour(filled, gridRes, gridRes);
  if (contour.length < 3) return null;

  // 网格 → 世界坐标
  const polygon: Point[] = contour.map(([gx, gy]) => {
    const wx = xMin + (gx / gridRes) * worldWidth;
    const wy = yMax - (gy / gridRes) * worldHeight;
    return { x: wx, y: wy };
  });

  // 计算包围盒
  let minX = Infinity, maxX = -Infinity, minY = Infinity, maxY = -Infinity;
  for (const p of polygon) {
    minX = Math.min(minX, p.x);
    maxX = Math.max(maxX, p.x);
    minY = Math.min(minY, p.y);
    maxY = Math.max(maxY, p.y);
  }

  return { polygon, bbox: { minX, maxX, minY, maxY } };
}

// ========== Marching Squares 相关函数（与原实现相同，保持稳定） ==========
const MARCHING_SQUARES_EDGES: Record<number, [number, number][]> = {
  0: [], 1: [[0,1]], 2: [[1,2]], 3: [[0,2]], 4: [[2,3]],
  5: [[0,1],[2,3]], 6: [[1,3]], 7: [[0,3]], 8: [[0,3]],
  9: [[1,3]], 10: [[0,1],[2,3]], 11: [[2,3]], 12: [[0,2]],
  13: [[1,2]], 14: [[0,1]], 15: [],
};

function polygonizeContour(segments: [number, number][], width: number): [number, number][] {
  if (segments.length === 0) return [];
  const adj = new Map<string, [number, number][]>();
  const addEdge = (a: [number, number], b: [number, number]) => {
    const key = `${a[0]},${a[1]}`;
    if (!adj.has(key)) adj.set(key, []);
    adj.get(key)!.push(b);
  };
  for (const [s, e] of segments) {
    const p1 = [s & 1, s >> 1] as [number, number];
    const p2 = [e & 1, e >> 1] as [number, number];
    addEdge(p1, p2);
    addEdge(p2, p1);
  }
  if (adj.size === 0) return [];
  const startKey = Array.from(adj.keys())[0];
  const start = startKey.split(',').map(Number) as [number, number];
  const polygon: [number, number][] = [start];
  let current = start;
  let prev: [number, number] | null = null;
  const visited = new Set<string>(startKey);
  while (true) {
    const neighbors = adj.get(`${current[0]},${current[1]}`) || [];
    let next: [number, number] | null = null;
    for (const n of neighbors) {
      const key = `${n[0]},${n[1]}`;
      if (!prev || (n[0] !== prev[0] || n[1] !== prev[1])) {
        next = n;
        break;
      }
    }
    if (!next) break;
    const nextKey = `${next[0]},${next[1]}`;
    if (visited.has(nextKey)) {
      if (next[0] === start[0] && next[1] === start[1]) break;
      else break;
    }
    visited.add(nextKey);
    polygon.push(next);
    prev = current;
    current = next;
  }
  return polygon;
}

function extractContour(filled: boolean[][], width: number, height: number): [number, number][] {
  if (width < 2 || height < 2) return [];
  const edgeSegments: [number, number][] = [];
  for (let y = 0; y < height - 1; y++) {
    for (let x = 0; x < width - 1; x++) {
      const idx = (filled[y][x] ? 1 : 0)
                | (filled[y][x+1] ? 2 : 0)
                | (filled[y+1][x+1] ? 4 : 0)
                | (filled[y+1][x] ? 8 : 0);
      const edges = MARCHING_SQUARES_EDGES[idx];
      if (!edges) continue;
      for (const [e1, e2] of edges) {
        const v1 = (() => {
          if (e1 === 0) return [x, y];
          if (e1 === 1) return [x+1, y];
          if (e1 === 2) return [x+1, y+1];
          return [x, y+1];
        })();
        const v2 = (() => {
          if (e2 === 0) return [x, y];
          if (e2 === 1) return [x+1, y];
          if (e2 === 2) return [x+1, y+1];
          return [x, y+1];
        })();
        edgeSegments.push([v1[0] + v1[1] * width, v2[0] + v2[1] * width]);
      }
    }
  }
  return polygonizeContour(edgeSegments, width);
}
现在能处理「两个图形边的交界处」吗？
能。因为我们将所有图形的线段合并后统一构建阻塞矩阵，任何由这些线段围成的封闭区域（无论形状简单还是复杂）都会被 BFS 精确填充，轮廓提取得到的就是该区域的多边形边界。

例如：

两个矩形相交，中间形成一个“十字”空洞，点击空洞内部 → 得到空洞的轮廓多边形。

一个圆形被一条直线穿过，点击左侧半圆区域 → 得到左侧半圆的轮廓。

注意事项
需要确保你的 segmentCache.ts 已经为所有曲线图形（圆、贝塞尔）生成了足够密集的线段采样（你已有 64 条边，已经足够）。

如果某个封闭区域非常细小（小于 2-3 个网格单元），可能会被过滤（fillCount < 6）。可以适当降低该阈值。

现在，你的注释工具将能够为任意由图形边界围成的封闭区域生成精确的多边形注释。















注释功能彻底重构方案（点注释 + 区域注释，基于 polygon-clipping）
目标
点注释：点击任意位置直接添加文本注释，与图形无关。

区域注释：基于图形闭合区域（由矩形、圆形、三角形、闭合贝塞尔曲线、闭合画笔等组合而成）检测点击所在的区域，在该区域上添加注释。区域可以带孔（如环形区域）。

区域检测核心：每个图形在创建/更新时生成近似多边形（世界坐标闭合点集），利用 polygon-clipping 库对整个图层的所有图形多边形进行布尔运算，计算出所有独立区域（带孔多边形）。点击时根据鼠标位置查找所属区域，创建注释。

一、新增依赖
bash
npm install polygon-clipping
二、类型定义重构（types.ts）
typescript
// types.ts

export interface Point {
  x: number;
  y: number;
}

export type ToolType = 
  | 'select' | 'point' | 'line' | 'rectangle' | 'circle' | 'triangle' 
  | 'quadratic' | 'brush' | 'eraser'
  | 'pointAnnotation'   // 点注释工具
  | 'regionAnnotation'; // 区域注释工具

// 图形扩展：增加 approximatePolygon（用于区域检测）
export interface Shape {
  id: string;
  layerId: string;
  groupId: string;
  type: 'point' | 'line' | 'rectangle' | 'circle' | 'triangle' | 'quadratic' | 'brush';
  points: Point[];
  approximatePolygon: Point[]; // 闭合多边形（世界坐标），开放曲线则为空数组
  color: string;
  // ... 其他原有属性（如 annotation 等保留，但点注释不再使用此字段）
}

// 点注释（独立）
export interface PointAnnotation {
  id: string;
  text: string;
  position: Point;      // 世界坐标
  layerId: string;
  createdAt: number;
  updatedAt: number;
}

// 区域注释（独立）
export interface RegionAnnotation {
  id: string;
  text: string;
  polygon: Point[][];   // 多环多边形：第一个环为外环，后续为内洞
  layerId: string;
  createdAt: number;
  updatedAt: number;
}

// 其他原有类型（Group, Layer, AxisConfig, GridConfig, LayerVisibility 等保持不变）
三、近似多边形生成工具（utils/approximatePolygon.ts）
为每种图形类型生成用于区域检测的闭合点集（世界坐标）。只有闭合图形才生成有效多边形，开放曲线（线段、开放贝塞尔、开放画笔）返回 []。

typescript
// utils/approximatePolygon.ts
import { Point, Shape } from '../types';

/**
 * 判断图形是否闭合（首尾点距离小于阈值）
 */
function isClosedShape(shape: Shape): boolean {
  if (shape.type === 'rectangle' || shape.type === 'circle' || shape.type === 'triangle') {
    return true;
  }
  if (shape.type === 'quadratic' || shape.type === 'brush') {
    if (shape.points.length < 3) return false;
    const first = shape.points[0];
    const last = shape.points[shape.points.length - 1];
    return Math.hypot(first.x - last.x, first.y - last.y) < 1e-6;
  }
  return false;
}

export function computeApproximatePolygon(shape: Shape): Point[] {
  if (!isClosedShape(shape)) return [];

  switch (shape.type) {
    case 'rectangle': {
      const [p1, p2] = shape.points;
      return [
        { x: p1.x, y: p1.y },
        { x: p2.x, y: p1.y },
        { x: p2.x, y: p2.y },
        { x: p1.x, y: p2.y },
        { x: p1.x, y: p1.y }, // 闭合
      ];
    }
    case 'circle': {
      const center = shape.points[0];
      const radius = Math.hypot(shape.points[1].x - center.x, shape.points[1].y - center.y);
      const segments = 48; // 足够平滑
      const points: Point[] = [];
      for (let i = 0; i <= segments; i++) {
        const angle = (i / segments) * Math.PI * 2;
        points.push({
          x: center.x + radius * Math.cos(angle),
          y: center.y + radius * Math.sin(angle),
        });
      }
      return points;
    }
    case 'triangle': {
      const [p1, p2, p3] = shape.points;
      return [p1, p2, p3, p1];
    }
    case 'quadratic': {
      // 二次贝塞尔曲线，采样足够多点构成闭合多边形
      const [p0, p1, ctrl] = shape.points; // p0起点, p1终点, ctrl控制点
      const segments = 40;
      const points: Point[] = [];
      for (let i = 0; i <= segments; i++) {
        const t = i / segments;
        const mt = 1 - t;
        const x = mt * mt * p0.x + 2 * mt * t * ctrl.x + t * t * p1.x;
        const y = mt * mt * p0.y + 2 * mt * t * ctrl.y + t * t * p1.y;
        points.push({ x, y });
      }
      return points; // 首尾已闭合（首尾点相同或接近）
    }
    case 'brush': {
      // 画笔闭合路径：直接使用所有点，并闭合
      const points = [...shape.points];
      if (points.length < 3) return [];
      // 确保首尾连接
      const first = points[0];
      const last = points[points.length - 1];
      if (Math.hypot(first.x - last.x, first.y - last.y) > 1e-6) {
        points.push(first);
      }
      return points;
    }
    default:
      return [];
  }
}
四、区域检测模块（utils/regionDetection.ts）
基于 polygon-clipping 计算图层内所有图形多边形划分出的独立区域。

typescript
// utils/regionDetection.ts
import polygonClipping from 'polygon-clipping';
import { Point } from '../types';

/**
 * 将 Point[] 转换为 polygon-clipping 接受的格式: [[[x,y], [x,y], ...]]
 */
function toClippingFormat(polygon: Point[]): number[][][] {
  return [polygon.map(p => [p.x, p.y])];
}

/**
 * 计算图层所有近似多边形划分出的独立区域（背景区域）。
 * 算法：求出所有多边形的并集，然后用世界边界矩形减去该并集，得到剩余背景区域（每个区域可能带孔）。
 * @param polygons 每个多边形是闭合点集（Point[]），要求简单多边形（不自交）。
 * @param worldBounds 世界坐标系边界 { xMin, xMax, yMin, yMax }
 * @returns 区域列表，每个区域是 Point[][]（多环：第一个环外环，后续内洞）
 */
export function computeRegionsFromPolygons(
  polygons: Point[][],
  worldBounds: { xMin: number; xMax: number; yMin: number; yMax: number }
): Point[][][] {
  if (polygons.length === 0) {
    // 无图形时，整个边界为一个区域（无洞）
    const boundingBox: Point[] = [
      { x: worldBounds.xMin, y: worldBounds.yMin },
      { x: worldBounds.xMax, y: worldBounds.yMin },
      { x: worldBounds.xMax, y: worldBounds.yMax },
      { x: worldBounds.xMin, y: worldBounds.yMax },
      { x: worldBounds.xMin, y: worldBounds.yMin },
    ];
    return [[boundingBox]];
  }

  // 转换所有多边形到 clipping 格式
  const clippingPolys = polygons.map(poly => toClippingFormat(poly));
  
  // 计算所有图形的并集
  const union = polygonClipping.union(...clippingPolys);
  
  // 世界边界矩形（逆时针）
  const boundingBox: number[][][] = [[
    [worldBounds.xMin, worldBounds.yMin],
    [worldBounds.xMax, worldBounds.yMin],
    [worldBounds.xMax, worldBounds.yMax],
    [worldBounds.xMin, worldBounds.yMax],
    [worldBounds.xMin, worldBounds.yMin],
  ]];
  
  // 边界矩形减去并集 = 背景区域（可能多个，带孔）
  const difference = polygonClipping.difference(boundingBox, union);
  
  // 转换回 Point[][][] 格式
  return difference.map(poly => poly.map(ring => ring.map(p => ({ x: p[0], y: p[1] }))));
}

/**
 * 射线法判断点是否在多边形内（含边界）
 */
export function isPointInPolygon(point: Point, polygon: Point[]): boolean {
  let inside = false;
  for (let i = 0, j = polygon.length - 1; i < polygon.length; j = i++) {
    const xi = polygon[i].x, yi = polygon[i].y;
    const xj = polygon[j].x, yj = polygon[j].y;
    const intersect = ((yi > point.y) !== (yj > point.y)) &&
      (point.x < (xj - xi) * (point.y - yi) / (yj - yi) + xi);
    if (intersect) inside = !inside;
  }
  return inside;
}

/**
 * 根据点击点找到所在区域（背景区域，排除内洞）
 * @param point 世界坐标点
 * @param regions 区域列表，每个区域是多环多边形 Point[][]
 * @returns 命中的区域多边形（多环），未命中返回 null
 */
export function findRegionByPoint(
  point: Point,
  regions: Point[][][]
): Point[][] | null {
  for (const region of regions) {
    if (region.length === 0) continue;
    const outerRing = region[0];
    if (!isPointInPolygon(point, outerRing)) continue;
    // 检查是否落在内洞中
    let insideHole = false;
    for (let i = 1; i < region.length; i++) {
      if (isPointInPolygon(point, region[i])) {
        insideHole = true;
        break;
      }
    }
    if (!insideHole) return region;
  }
  return null;
}
五、Store 重构（useAppStore.ts）
增加点注释和区域注释的状态管理，以及区域缓存。

typescript
// useAppStore.ts 修改部分（仅展示新增/修改的片段）

import { computeApproximatePolygon } from '../utils/approximatePolygon';
import { computeRegionsFromPolygons } from '../utils/regionDetection';

interface AppState {
  // ... 原有状态

  // 点注释
  pointAnnotations: PointAnnotation[];
  addPointAnnotation: (annotation: Omit<PointAnnotation, 'id' | 'createdAt' | 'updatedAt'>) => void;
  updatePointAnnotation: (id: string, text: string) => void;
  removePointAnnotation: (id: string) => void;
  clearPointAnnotations: () => void;

  // 区域注释
  regionAnnotations: RegionAnnotation[];
  addRegionAnnotation: (annotation: Omit<RegionAnnotation, 'id' | 'createdAt' | 'updatedAt'>) => void;
  updateRegionAnnotation: (id: string, text: string) => void;
  removeRegionAnnotation: (id: string) => void;
  clearRegionAnnotations: () => void;

  // 区域检测缓存
  regionPolygonsCache: Record<string, Point[][][]>; // key: layerId, value: 区域列表
  refreshRegionCache: (layerId: string) => void;
}

export const useAppStore = create<AppState>((set, get) => ({
  // ... 原有初始化

  pointAnnotations: [],
  addPointAnnotation: (annotation) =>
    set((state) => ({
      pointAnnotations: [
        ...state.pointAnnotations,
        {
          ...annotation,
          id: `point_anno_${Date.now()}_${Math.random()}`,
          createdAt: Date.now(),
          updatedAt: Date.now(),
        },
      ],
    })),
  updatePointAnnotation: (id, text) =>
    set((state) => ({
      pointAnnotations: state.pointAnnotations.map(a =>
        a.id === id ? { ...a, text, updatedAt: Date.now() } : a
      ),
    })),
  removePointAnnotation: (id) =>
    set((state) => ({
      pointAnnotations: state.pointAnnotations.filter(a => a.id !== id),
    })),
  clearPointAnnotations: () => set({ pointAnnotations: [] }),

  regionAnnotations: [],
  addRegionAnnotation: (annotation) =>
    set((state) => ({
      regionAnnotations: [
        ...state.regionAnnotations,
        {
          ...annotation,
          id: `region_anno_${Date.now()}_${Math.random()}`,
          createdAt: Date.now(),
          updatedAt: Date.now(),
        },
      ],
    })),
  updateRegionAnnotation: (id, text) =>
    set((state) => ({
      regionAnnotations: state.regionAnnotations.map(a =>
        a.id === id ? { ...a, text, updatedAt: Date.now() } : a
      ),
    })),
  removeRegionAnnotation: (id) =>
    set((state) => ({
      regionAnnotations: state.regionAnnotations.filter(a => a.id !== id),
    })),
  clearRegionAnnotations: () => set({ regionAnnotations: [] }),

  regionPolygonsCache: {},
  refreshRegionCache: (layerId) => {
    const state = get();
    // 收集当前图层所有有效近似多边形（非空数组）
    const polygons = state.shapes
      .filter(s => s.layerId === layerId && s.approximatePolygon.length > 0)
      .map(s => s.approximatePolygon);
    const worldBounds = {
      xMin: state.axis.xMin,
      xMax: state.axis.xMax,
      yMin: state.axis.yMin,
      yMax: state.axis.yMax,
    };
    const regions = computeRegionsFromPolygons(polygons, worldBounds);
    set((s) => ({
      regionPolygonsCache: { ...s.regionPolygonsCache, [layerId]: regions },
    }));
  },

  // 修改 addShape / updateShape / removeShape 时自动更新近似多边形并刷新缓存
  addShape: (shape) =>
    set((state) => {
      const approximatePolygon = computeApproximatePolygon(shape);
      const newShape = { ...shape, approximatePolygon };
      const newShapes = [...state.shapes, newShape];
      // 刷新该图层的区域缓存
      setTimeout(() => {
        get().refreshRegionCache(shape.layerId);
      }, 0);
      return { shapes: newShapes };
    }),
  updateShape: (id, updates) =>
    set((state) => {
      const oldShape = state.shapes.find(s => s.id === id);
      if (!oldShape) return state;
      const updatedShape = { ...oldShape, ...updates };
      const approximatePolygon = computeApproximatePolygon(updatedShape);
      const newShape = { ...updatedShape, approximatePolygon };
      const newShapes = state.shapes.map(s => s.id === id ? newShape : s);
      // 刷新缓存
      setTimeout(() => {
        get().refreshRegionCache(newShape.layerId);
      }, 0);
      return { shapes: newShapes };
    }),
  removeShape: (id) =>
    set((state) => {
      const shape = state.shapes.find(s => s.id === id);
      const newShapes = state.shapes.filter(s => s.id !== id);
      if (shape) {
        setTimeout(() => {
          get().refreshRegionCache(shape.layerId);
        }, 0);
      }
      return { shapes: newShapes };
    }),

  // 图层切换时也需要刷新缓存（在 setActiveLayer 中调用 refreshRegionCache）
  setActiveLayer: (id) =>
    set((state) => {
      if (id) {
        setTimeout(() => {
          get().refreshRegionCache(id);
        }, 0);
      }
      return { activeLayerId: id };
    }),

  // ... 其他原有代码
}));
六、画布交互重构（MainCanvas.tsx）
修改鼠标事件，处理点注释和区域注释工具；绘制时分别绘制点注释和区域注释。

typescript
// MainCanvas.tsx 关键修改

import { computeApproximatePolygon } from '../utils/approximatePolygon';
import { findRegionByPoint } from '../utils/regionDetection';
import { PointAnnotation, RegionAnnotation } from '../types';

// 在组件内部添加编辑器状态（点注释和区域注释分开）
const [pointAnnotationEditor, setPointAnnotationEditor] = useState<{
  editorId: string;
  x: number;
  y: number;
  annotationId: string | null;
  existingText: string;
  position: Point;
} | null>(null);

const [regionAnnotationEditor, setRegionAnnotationEditor] = useState<{
  editorId: string;
  x: number;
  y: number;
  annotationId: string | null;
  existingText: string;
  polygon: Point[][];
} | null>(null);

// 获取 store 中的新状态
const {
  // ... 原有
  pointAnnotations,
  addPointAnnotation,
  updatePointAnnotation,
  removePointAnnotation,
  regionAnnotations,
  addRegionAnnotation,
  updateRegionAnnotation,
  removeRegionAnnotation,
  regionPolygonsCache,
  refreshRegionCache,
} = useAppStore();

// 处理鼠标按下
const handleMouseDown = useCallback((e: React.MouseEvent) => {
  // ... 平移/其他工具前置判断

  const coords = getCanvasCoords(e);
  const worldCoords = canvasToWorldFn(coords.x, coords.y);

  // 点注释工具
  if (currentTool === 'pointAnnotation') {
    setPointAnnotationEditor({
      editorId: generateEditorId(),
      x: e.clientX,
      y: e.clientY,
      annotationId: null,
      existingText: '',
      position: worldCoords,
    });
    return;
  }

  // 区域注释工具
  if (currentTool === 'regionAnnotation') {
    const regions = regionPolygonsCache[activeLayerId] || [];
    const hitRegion = findRegionByPoint(worldCoords, regions);
    if (hitRegion) {
      setRegionAnnotationEditor({
        editorId: generateEditorId(),
        x: e.clientX,
        y: e.clientY,
        annotationId: null,
        existingText: '',
        polygon: hitRegion,
      });
    } else {
      // 可选：提示没有区域
      console.log('未点击到任何区域');
    }
    return;
  }

  // ... 原有绘图工具逻辑
}, [/* 依赖项 */]);

// 绘制函数中增加点注释和区域注释的绘制
const drawCanvas = useCallback(() => {
  // ... 原有绘制代码

  // 绘制点注释
  pointAnnotations.forEach(anno => {
    if (anno.layerId !== activeLayerId) return;
    const canvasPos = worldToCanvasFn(anno.position.x, anno.position.y);
    ctx.save();
    ctx.fillStyle = '#ff4d4f';
    ctx.shadowBlur = 0;
    ctx.beginPath();
    ctx.arc(canvasPos.x, canvasPos.y, 6, 0, 2 * Math.PI);
    ctx.fill();
    ctx.fillStyle = '#fff';
    ctx.font = 'bold 12px sans-serif';
    ctx.textAlign = 'center';
    ctx.textBaseline = 'middle';
    ctx.fillText('📍', canvasPos.x, canvasPos.y);
    ctx.fillStyle = '#333';
    ctx.font = '12px sans-serif';
    ctx.textAlign = 'left';
    ctx.fillText(anno.text.length > 15 ? anno.text.slice(0, 12) + '...' : anno.text, canvasPos.x + 10, canvasPos.y - 5);
    ctx.restore();
  });

  // 绘制区域注释
  regionAnnotations.forEach(anno => {
    if (anno.layerId !== activeLayerId) return;
    ctx.save();
    ctx.fillStyle = 'rgba(24, 144, 255, 0.2)';
    ctx.strokeStyle = '#1890ff';
    ctx.lineWidth = 2;
    for (const ring of anno.polygon) {
      if (ring.length < 3) continue;
      const canvasRing = ring.map(p => worldToCanvasFn(p.x, p.y));
      ctx.beginPath();
      ctx.moveTo(canvasRing[0].x, canvasRing[0].y);
      for (let i = 1; i < canvasRing.length; i++) {
        ctx.lineTo(canvasRing[i].x, canvasRing[i].y);
      }
      ctx.closePath();
      ctx.fill();
      ctx.stroke();
    }
    // 在区域左上角显示文本（取外环最小点）
    const outerRing = anno.polygon[0];
    let minX = Infinity, minY = Infinity;
    for (const p of outerRing) {
      if (p.x < minX) minX = p.x;
      if (p.y < minY) minY = p.y;
    }
    const labelPos = worldToCanvasFn(minX, minY);
    ctx.fillStyle = '#333';
    ctx.font = '12px sans-serif';
    ctx.shadowBlur = 0;
    ctx.fillText(anno.text.length > 20 ? anno.text.slice(0, 17) + '...' : anno.text, labelPos.x + 5, labelPos.y - 5);
    ctx.restore();
  });

  // ... 原有绘制代码
}, [/* 依赖项包括 pointAnnotations, regionAnnotations, regionPolygonsCache 等 */]);

// 编辑器保存逻辑（点注释）
const handlePointAnnotationSave = useCallback((text: string) => {
  const editor = pointAnnotationEditor;
  if (!editor || editor.editorId !== currentEditorIdRef.current) return;
  if (editor.annotationId) {
    updatePointAnnotation(editor.annotationId, text);
  } else {
    addPointAnnotation({
      text,
      position: editor.position,
      layerId: activeLayerId || layers[0]?.id,
    });
  }
  saveHistory();
  setPointAnnotationEditor(null);
}, [pointAnnotationEditor, updatePointAnnotation, addPointAnnotation, activeLayerId, layers]);

// 区域注释保存
const handleRegionAnnotationSave = useCallback((text: string) => {
  const editor = regionAnnotationEditor;
  if (!editor || editor.editorId !== currentEditorIdRef.current) return;
  if (editor.annotationId) {
    updateRegionAnnotation(editor.annotationId, text);
  } else {
    addRegionAnnotation({
      text,
      polygon: editor.polygon,
      layerId: activeLayerId || layers[0]?.id,
    });
  }
  saveHistory();
  setRegionAnnotationEditor(null);
}, [regionAnnotationEditor, updateRegionAnnotation, addRegionAnnotation, activeLayerId, layers]);

// 在 JSX 中渲染两个不同的编辑器弹窗（可以复用同一个 AnnotationEditor 组件，但传递不同保存回调）
{pointAnnotationEditor && (
  <AnnotationEditor
    x={pointAnnotationEditor.x}
    y={pointAnnotationEditor.y}
    annotationId={pointAnnotationEditor.annotationId}
    existingText={pointAnnotationEditor.existingText}
    onSave={handlePointAnnotationSave}
    onCancel={() => setPointAnnotationEditor(null)}
  />
)}
{regionAnnotationEditor && (
  <AnnotationEditor
    x={regionAnnotationEditor.x}
    y={regionAnnotationEditor.y}
    annotationId={regionAnnotationEditor.annotationId}
    existingText={regionAnnotationEditor.existingText}
    onSave={handleRegionAnnotationSave}
    onCancel={() => setRegionAnnotationEditor(null)}
  />
)}
七、工具栏增加两个按钮（Toolbar.tsx）
typescript
const tools: { type: ToolType; icon: string; label: string }[] = [
  { type: 'select', icon: '⬚', label: '选择' },
  { type: 'point', icon: '•', label: '点' },
  { type: 'line', icon: '/', label: '线段' },
  { type: 'rectangle', icon: '□', label: '矩形' },
  { type: 'circle', icon: '○', label: '圆形' },
  { type: 'triangle', icon: '△', label: '三角形' },
  { type: 'quadratic', icon: '⌒', label: '贝塞尔' },
  { type: 'brush', icon: '✎', label: '画笔' },
  { type: 'eraser', icon: '✕', label: '橡皮' },
  { type: 'pointAnnotation', icon: '📍', label: '点注释' },
  { type: 'regionAnnotation', icon: '🔲', label: '区域注释' },
];
八、贝塞尔曲线与其他图形组合区域识别的保证
贝塞尔曲线闭合判断：用户绘制二次贝塞尔曲线时，通常首尾点自动闭合（双击或自动闭合逻辑）。在 computeApproximatePolygon 中，对于 quadratic 类型，我们默认它是闭合的（由交互保证）。如果实际未闭合，近似多边形将是一条开放折线，此时不应参与区域检测。可以在 isClosedShape 中增加距离阈值判断，若首尾距离大于 1e-6 则视为开放，返回空数组。

采样精度：quadratic 采样 40 个点，足够平滑，且不会自交（二次贝塞尔曲线无自交）。

图形组合：polygon-clipping 能够正确处理多个多边形的并集、差集，无论这些多边形是矩形、圆形（多边形近似）、三角形、闭合贝塞尔曲线、闭合画笔路径。最终得到的背景区域（边界矩形减去并集）能够正确反映由这些图形围出来的独立区域（包括带孔区域）。

开放曲线：线段、开放画笔、开放贝塞尔曲线不产生近似多边形，因此不会影响区域划分。这符合预期，因为它们不封闭，不定义区域边界。

图层独立：每个图层的区域缓存独立，注释只属于其所在图层。

九、迁移与兼容
原有的 annotations 数组可以保留并逐步废弃，或者直接删除。为了平滑过渡，可以在加载旧数据时，将旧注释转换为点注释（因为旧注释本质上是点注释或基于图形的注释，但为了简化，统一转为点注释，位置取几何中心）。

需要清理原有 Shape 上的 annotation 字段，不再使用。

十、总结
通过上述重构：

点注释与区域注释完全分离，职责清晰。

区域注释基于精确的多边形布尔运算，能够正确处理由矩形、圆形、三角形、闭合贝塞尔曲线、闭合画笔等任意组合形成的复杂区域（包括带孔区域）。

每个图形在创建/更新时自动生成近似多边形，并触发区域缓存刷新，确保区域检测实时准确。

性能良好，区域缓存按图层存储，只在图形变化时重新计算。

注意：需要确保 polygon-clipping 库的类型定义可用（如安装 @types/polygon-clipping）。此外，对于超大点数（如画笔数千点）的近似多边形，布尔运算可能稍慢，但一般场景下可接受。如有性能问题，可对画笔路径进行简化（Ramer-Douglas-Peucker 算法）后再存入 approximatePolygon。








我们将采用 Moore邻域边界追踪 算法直接提取连通区域的外轮廓，然后用 Douglas-Peucker 算法简化多边形，替代原来的凸包方案。
这样可以得到完全贴合墙体内壁、支持凹形的精细边界，且代码量可控，不依赖额外几何库。

下面给出完整的新版 regionDetectionBySampling.ts 文件内容，可直接替换原文件。

typescript
// src/utils/regionDetectionBySampling.ts
import { Point, Shape } from '../types';
import { pointToShapeDistance } from './shapeDistance';  // 已有实现

// ---------- 轮廓提取（Moore邻域边界追踪）----------
interface GridPoint {
  i: number; // 行索引 (y方向)
  j: number; // 列索引 (x方向)
}

/**
 * 从一组属于同一连通区域的前景点（网格坐标）中提取最外层轮廓
 * @param points 前景点的世界坐标数组（每个点包含原始坐标x,y）
 * @param stepX 网格x方向步长
 * @param stepY 网格y方向步长
 * @param xMin 世界坐标x最小值
 * @param yMin 世界坐标y最小值
 * @returns 轮廓多边形（世界坐标，未简化）
 */
function extractRawBoundary(
  points: Point[],
  stepX: number,
  stepY: number,
  xMin: number,
  yMin: number
): Point[] {
  if (points.length < 3) return [];

  // 1. 建立前景集合（使用字符串索引）
  const foregroundSet = new Set<string>();
  // 同时记录每个点的网格索引（四舍五入取整，避免浮点误差）
  const gridPoints: GridPoint[] = [];

  for (const p of points) {
    // 计算网格索引（由于采样时就是按步长生成的，可以直接反算）
    let i = Math.round((p.y - yMin) / stepY);
    let j = Math.round((p.x - xMin) / stepX);
    // 边界保护
    i = Math.min(Math.max(i, 0), Math.floor((points[0].y - yMin) / stepY) + 10);
    j = Math.min(Math.max(j, 0), Math.floor((points[0].x - xMin) / stepX) + 10);
    const key = `${i},${j}`;
    if (!foregroundSet.has(key)) {
      foregroundSet.add(key);
      gridPoints.push({ i, j });
    }
  }

  if (gridPoints.length < 3) return [];

  // 2. 找到最下最左的边界点（i最大，j最小）
  let start: GridPoint | null = null;
  for (const gp of gridPoints) {
    // 判断是否为边界点：4邻域中有背景（或超出网格范围）
    const isBoundary =
      !foregroundSet.has(`${gp.i-1},${gp.j}`) ||
      !foregroundSet.has(`${gp.i+1},${gp.j}`) ||
      !foregroundSet.has(`${gp.i},${gp.j-1}`) ||
      !foregroundSet.has(`${gp.i},${gp.j+1}`);
    if (!isBoundary) continue;
    if (start === null || gp.i > start.i || (gp.i === start.i && gp.j < start.j)) {
      start = gp;
    }
  }
  if (!start) return [];

  // 3. Moore邻域方向（8方向，逆时针顺序）
  const dirs = [
    { di: -1, dj:  0 }, // 上
    { di: -1, dj:  1 }, // 右上
    { di:  0, dj:  1 }, // 右
    { di:  1, dj:  1 }, // 右下
    { di:  1, dj:  0 }, // 下
    { di:  1, dj: -1 }, // 左下
    { di:  0, dj: -1 }, // 左
    { di: -1, dj: -1 }, // 左上
  ];

  const boundary: GridPoint[] = [];
  let current = start;
  let prevDir = 6; // 从方向“左”开始试探（可任意，算法会自动适应）
  do {
    boundary.push(current);
    // 从 (prevDir + 7) % 8 方向开始顺时针搜索下一个边界点
    let found = false;
    for (let k = 0; k < 8; k++) {
      const dirIdx = (prevDir + 7 + k) % 8; // 顺时针旋转
      const nb = { i: current.i + dirs[dirIdx].di, j: current.j + dirs[dirIdx].dj };
      if (foregroundSet.has(`${nb.i},${nb.j}`)) {
        // 检查该邻域点是否也是边界点（确保追踪轮廓）
        const isNbBoundary =
          !foregroundSet.has(`${nb.i-1},${nb.j}`) ||
          !foregroundSet.has(`${nb.i+1},${nb.j}`) ||
          !foregroundSet.has(`${nb.i},${nb.j-1}`) ||
          !foregroundSet.has(`${nb.i},${nb.j+1}`);
        if (isNbBoundary) {
          current = nb;
          prevDir = dirIdx;
          found = true;
          break;
        }
      }
    }
    if (!found) break; // 无法继续（理论上不会发生）
  } while (!(current.i === start.i && current.j === start.j) && boundary.length < 10000);

  if (boundary.length < 3) return [];

  // 4. 将网格索引转换为世界坐标
  const worldBoundary = boundary.map(gp => ({
    x: xMin + gp.j * stepX,
    y: yMin + gp.i * stepY,
  }));

  return worldBoundary;
}

// ---------- 多边形简化（Douglas-Peucker）----------
function douglasPeucker(points: Point[], epsilon: number): Point[] {
  if (points.length < 3) return points.slice();

  // 找到最远点
  let maxDist = 0;
  let index = 0;
  const start = points[0];
  const end = points[points.length - 1];
  for (let i = 1; i < points.length - 1; i++) {
    const dist = perpendicularDistance(points[i], start, end);
    if (dist > maxDist) {
      maxDist = dist;
      index = i;
    }
  }

  if (maxDist > epsilon) {
    const left = douglasPeucker(points.slice(0, index + 1), epsilon);
    const right = douglasPeucker(points.slice(index), epsilon);
    return left.slice(0, -1).concat(right);
  } else {
    return [start, end];
  }
}

function perpendicularDistance(p: Point, a: Point, b: Point): number {
  const area = Math.abs((b.x - a.x) * (p.y - a.y) - (b.y - a.y) * (p.x - a.x));
  const len = Math.hypot(b.x - a.x, b.y - a.y);
  return len === 0 ? Math.hypot(p.x - a.x, p.y - a.y) : area / len;
}

// ---------- 主函数：采样 + 连通区域 + 轮廓提取 ----------
export function detectClosedRegionsBySampling(
  shapes: Shape[],
  worldBounds: { xMin: number; xMax: number; yMin: number; yMax: number },
  resolution: number = 150,
  wallDistanceThreshold?: number
): Point[][] {
  const { xMin, xMax, yMin, yMax } = worldBounds;
  const width = xMax - xMin;
  const height = yMax - yMin;
  const diag = Math.hypot(width, height);
  const threshold = wallDistanceThreshold ?? diag * 0.005; // 墙厚度阈值

  const stepX = width / (resolution - 1);
  const stepY = height / (resolution - 1);

  // 辅助：网格索引 -> 世界坐标
  const gridToWorld = (i: number, j: number): Point => ({
    x: xMin + j * stepX,
    y: yMin + i * stepY,
  });

  // 1. 标记墙（wallGrid[i][j] = true）
  const wallGrid: boolean[][] = Array(resolution).fill(null).map(() => Array(resolution).fill(false));
  for (let i = 0; i < resolution; i++) {
    for (let j = 0; j < resolution; j++) {
      const worldPoint = gridToWorld(i, j);
      let minDist = Infinity;
      for (const shape of shapes) {
        const dist = pointToShapeDistance(worldPoint, shape);
        if (dist < minDist) minDist = dist;
        if (minDist < threshold) break;
      }
      if (minDist < threshold) wallGrid[i][j] = true;
    }
  }

  // 2. 连通区域标记（BFS，4邻域）
  const visited: boolean[][] = Array(resolution).fill(null).map(() => Array(resolution).fill(false));
  const regionsPoints: Point[][] = []; // 每个区域存储其所有采样点（世界坐标）

  const bfs = (startI: number, startJ: number): Point[] => {
    const queue: [number, number][] = [[startI, startJ]];
    visited[startI][startJ] = true;
    const points: Point[] = [];

    while (queue.length) {
      const [i, j] = queue.shift()!;
      points.push(gridToWorld(i, j));

      const neighbors = [[-1,0],[1,0],[0,-1],[0,1]];
      for (const [di, dj] of neighbors) {
        const ni = i + di, nj = j + dj;
        if (ni >= 0 && ni < resolution && nj >= 0 && nj < resolution && !visited[ni][nj] && !wallGrid[ni][nj]) {
          visited[ni][nj] = true;
          queue.push([ni, nj]);
        }
      }
    }
    return points;
  };

  for (let i = 0; i < resolution; i++) {
    for (let j = 0; j < resolution; j++) {
      if (!wallGrid[i][j] && !visited[i][j]) {
        const regionPoints = bfs(i, j);

        // 检查是否触及边界（外部区域）
        let touchesBoundary = false;
        for (const p of regionPoints) {
          if (Math.abs(p.x - xMin) < stepX * 0.5 ||
              Math.abs(p.x - xMax) < stepX * 0.5 ||
              Math.abs(p.y - yMin) < stepY * 0.5 ||
              Math.abs(p.y - yMax) < stepY * 0.5) {
            touchesBoundary = true;
            break;
          }
        }
        if (!touchesBoundary && regionPoints.length >= 10) {
          regionsPoints.push(regionPoints);
        }
      }
    }
  }

  // 3. 对每个连通区域提取轮廓并简化
  const polygons: Point[][] = [];
  const simplifyEpsilon = Math.min(stepX, stepY) * 1.5; // 简化阈值（世界单位）

  for (const regionPoints of regionsPoints) {
    // 提取原始轮廓（网格边界）
    let boundary = extractRawBoundary(regionPoints, stepX, stepY, xMin, yMin);
    if (boundary.length < 3) continue;

    // 闭合轮廓（首尾应已闭合，但确保）
    if (Math.hypot(boundary[0].x - boundary[boundary.length-1].x, boundary[0].y - boundary[boundary.length-1].y) > stepX * 0.5) {
      boundary.push(boundary[0]);
    }

    // 简化多边形
    const simplified = douglasPeucker(boundary, simplifyEpsilon);
    if (simplified.length >= 3) {
      // 确保逆时针顺序（可选，大多数算法不依赖）
      polygons.push(simplified);
    }
  }

  return polygons;
}
配套的 shapeDistance.ts（保持不变，但确保与上述代码兼容）
typescript
// src/utils/shapeDistance.ts
import { Point, Shape } from '../types';

function distanceToLineSegment(p: Point, a: Point, b: Point): number {
  const ax = p.x - a.x, ay = p.y - a.y;
  const bx = b.x - a.x, by = b.y - a.y;
  const dot = ax * bx + ay * by;
  const len2 = bx * bx + by * by;
  if (len2 === 0) return Math.hypot(ax, ay);
  let t = dot / len2;
  t = Math.max(0, Math.min(1, t));
  const projX = a.x + t * bx;
  const projY = a.y + t * by;
  return Math.hypot(p.x - projX, p.y - projY);
}

function pointToQuadraticDistance(p: Point, p0: Point, p1: Point, ctrl: Point, segments = 30): number {
  let minDist = Infinity;
  for (let i = 0; i <= segments; i++) {
    const t = i / segments;
    const mt = 1 - t;
    const x = mt * mt * p0.x + 2 * mt * t * ctrl.x + t * t * p1.x;
    const y = mt * mt * p0.y + 2 * mt * t * ctrl.y + t * t * p1.y;
    const dist = Math.hypot(p.x - x, p.y - y);
    if (dist < minDist) minDist = dist;
  }
  return minDist;
}

export function pointToShapeDistance(point: Point, shape: Shape): number {
  const pts = shape.points;
  switch (shape.type) {
    case 'point':
      return pts.length > 0 ? Math.hypot(point.x - pts[0].x, point.y - pts[0].y) : Infinity;
    case 'line':
      if (pts.length >= 2) return distanceToLineSegment(point, pts[0], pts[1]);
      return Infinity;
    case 'rectangle':
      if (pts.length >= 2) {
        const p1 = pts[0], p2 = pts[1];
        const left = Math.min(p1.x, p2.x), right = Math.max(p1.x, p2.x);
        const bottom = Math.min(p1.y, p2.y), top = Math.max(p1.y, p2.y);
        const dx = Math.max(left - point.x, 0, point.x - right);
        const dy = Math.max(bottom - point.y, 0, point.y - top);
        return Math.hypot(dx, dy);
      }
      return Infinity;
    case 'circle':
      if (pts.length >= 2) {
        const center = pts[0];
        const radius = Math.hypot(pts[1].x - center.x, pts[1].y - center.y);
        return Math.abs(Math.hypot(point.x - center.x, point.y - center.y) - radius);
      }
      return Infinity;
    case 'triangle':
      if (pts.length >= 3) {
        const [a, b, c] = pts;
        const d1 = distanceToLineSegment(point, a, b);
        const d2 = distanceToLineSegment(point, b, c);
        const d3 = distanceToLineSegment(point, c, a);
        return Math.min(d1, d2, d3);
      }
      return Infinity;
    case 'quadratic':
      if (pts.length >= 3) {
        return pointToQuadraticDistance(point, pts[0], pts[1], pts[2]);
      }
      return Infinity;
    case 'brush':
      if (pts.length >= 2) {
        let minDist = Infinity;
        for (let i = 0; i < pts.length - 1; i++) {
          const dist = distanceToLineSegment(point, pts[i], pts[i+1]);
          if (dist < minDist) minDist = dist;
        }
        return minDist;
      } else if (pts.length === 1) {
        return Math.hypot(point.x - pts[0].x, point.y - pts[0].y);
      }
      return Infinity;
    default:
      return Infinity;
  }
}

若需要更高质量（如减少锯齿），可调整 resolution 参数（例如 200~300），以及 simplifyEpsilon（默认 stepX * 1.5）。

效果说明
完全保留凹形：L形、U形、回廊等内凹区域都能正确追踪出贴合墙体内壁的边界。

锯齿可控：通过Douglas-Peucker简化，自动删除共线或冗余点，同时平滑轻微锯齿。

性能稳定：每区域轮廓追踪复杂度 O(点数)，点数一般小于网格采样点数量（因只取边界点）。

无需外部库：纯TypeScript实现，可直接集成。

typescript
// 放在 useAppStore 文件顶部或内部
function shapeToLineSegments(shape: Shape): [Point, Point][] {
  const { type, points } = shape;
  const segments: [Point, Point][] = [];

  if (points.length === 0) return segments;

  switch (type) {
    case 'point':
      // 点没有线段，忽略
      break;
    case 'line':
      if (points.length >= 2) {
        segments.push([points[0], points[1]]);
      }
      break;
    case 'rectangle':
      if (points.length >= 2) {
        const p1 = points[0];
        const p2 = points[1];
        const minX = Math.min(p1.x, p2.x);
        const maxX = Math.max(p1.x, p2.x);
        const minY = Math.min(p1.y, p2.y);
        const maxY = Math.max(p1.y, p2.y);
        const a = { x: minX, y: minY };
        const b = { x: maxX, y: minY };
        const c = { x: maxX, y: maxY };
        const d = { x: minX, y: maxY };
        segments.push([a, b], [b, c], [c, d], [d, a]);
      }
      break;
    case 'circle':
      if (points.length >= 2) {
        const center = points[0];
        const radius = Math.hypot(points[1].x - center.x, points[1].y - center.y);
        const steps = 36;
        const verts: Point[] = [];
        for (let i = 0; i <= steps; i++) {
          const angle = (i / steps) * 2 * Math.PI;
          verts.push({
            x: center.x + radius * Math.cos(angle),
            y: center.y + radius * Math.sin(angle),
          });
        }
        for (let i = 0; i < verts.length - 1; i++) {
          segments.push([verts[i], verts[i+1]]);
        }
      }
      break;
    case 'triangle':
      if (points.length >= 3) {
        const [a, b, c] = points;
        segments.push([a, b], [b, c], [c, a]);
      }
      break;
    case 'quadratic':
      // 贝塞尔曲线需要采样为折线段
      if (points.length >= 3) {
        const [p0, p1, ctrl] = points;
        const steps = 30;
        const sampled: Point[] = [];
        for (let i = 0; i <= steps; i++) {
          const t = i / steps;
          const mt = 1 - t;
          const x = mt * mt * p0.x + 2 * mt * t * ctrl.x + t * t * p1.x;
          const y = mt * mt * p0.y + 2 * mt * t * ctrl.y + t * t * p1.y;
          sampled.push({ x, y });
        }
        for (let i = 0; i < sampled.length - 1; i++) {
          segments.push([sampled[i], sampled[i+1]]);
        }
      }
      break;
    case 'brush':
      // 画笔已经是连续点
      for (let i = 0; i < points.length - 1; i++) {
        segments.push([points[i], points[i+1]]);
      }
      break;
    default:
      // 其他情况，如果 points 数量足够则连成线段
      for (let i = 0; i < points.length - 1; i++) {
        segments.push([points[i], points[i+1]]);
      }
      break;
  }
  return segments;
}
